- **evidence-before-done**: 多助手协作底座。任何助手准备说「做好了」「修好了」「放好了」「没问题」「都对上了」， 或要交付、在进度里说某部分已完成、进入下一步、转述别的助手或子任务的「已完成」之前用： 先拿到对得上当前版本、范围、环境的有效证据（打开看、播放、重算、按原步骤再走一遍、逐条对照原要求）； 本人或指定核验人对同一版本的有效核验可复用，有变更或失败只补核受影响部分；执行人自己的报告不算独立核验。 只报进行到哪的普通进度不触发全套重验。证据撑得住才下结论，撑不住就照实报现状。 不用于：第三方独立复核清单（另用审核类技能）； 动手前讲方案（另用方案确认类技能）。与同仓 ../dispatch-accept-verify/ 分工：那支管收回来，本支管核过再宣称完成。 ([dabingfy-dot/solo-to-team-skills](https://github.com/dabingfy-dot/solo-to-team-skills/tree/HEAD/bundles/bundle1/packages/Solo-to-Team-deepseek-bundle1/foundation/skills/evidence-before-done) · ★1.1k · 5 copies)
- **evidence-before-done**: Blocks the agent from claiming a task is done, fixed, verified, ready, or complete unless that claim is backed by concrete evidence (a real command actually run, with its real output) or an explicit list of what was NOT verified. Best used reactively before presenting any completion claim to the user. Use when the user says "is it done?", "are you sure?", "did you test it?", "ship it", "mark this complete", or whenever the agent is about to claim a task is finished/fixed/working/verified. Also runs LAST as the aggregator when other proofguard guards fired, confirming their fix-required items… ([mohamedzhioua/proofguard](https://github.com/mohamedzhioua/proofguard/tree/HEAD/skills/evidence-before-done) · ★1)
